Windows packaged-app path resolution redirects both actual and expected LocalAppData paths to the same LocalCache location. No Core, GUI, backend, EXE, production data or credentials were started/accessed. Installed runtime/default-data requirements remain BLOCKED. Requirement 0 FAIL is not a claim that every historical or supplemental execution passed.

## Status and evidence rules

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| PENDING | Requirement has not yet been evidenced. A planned test or implemented control does not establish a pass. |
| PASS | The stated criterion was exercised or reviewed using the correct evidence class, with evidence path, actual result and date. |
| FAIL | An executed acceptance check demonstrates a defect or missing mandatory implementation. |
| UNSUPPORTED | The real backend explicitly does not offer this capability, supported by current official contract/discovery evidence. This does not waive mandatory product requirements. |
| BLOCKED | A required action cannot be performed because a specific prerequisite or approval is missing; record exact action, target, reason and next authorized path. |

For conditional backend features, distinguish two facts: the capability may be UNSUPPORTED, while the UI's accurate unsupported display can PASS. Dot send/receive/thread/history and simultaneous three-agent operation remain mandatory. A blocked or unsupported mandatory live capability prevents a Final-complete declaration even if all independent local tests pass. A section with mixed outcomes must show the unresolved outcome and link to its individual rows; never hide failures behind a percentage.

Evidence must record date, environment/toolchain version, isolated data/repo paths, test case, action, expected result, actual result, and sanitized file/screenshot/log references. For backend claims, include actual handshake/capability/session evidence. For UI claims, record real interaction and observed result. Test doubles must be named as such; their results count only toward the relevant local/core criterion. Never store authentication values or hidden reasoning as evidence.

The user's execution security directive takes precedence where §23 raw preservation conflicts with secret protection: redact before every display/persistence/export boundary, record that redaction occurred, and exclude hidden chain-of-thought. Preserve the remaining raw event faithfully. Do not use credential files or real secret values in tests.

## Planned test families

These identifiers map requirements to the intended verification method. They are a test plan, not a claim that a script or executed result already exists. Each criterion has a unique case ID `T-<requirement ID>`; when a check is automated, attach its real test file/function or command to the Evidence column. Integration cases can satisfy several rows only when every row's outcome is explicitly covered.

| Family | Verification | Scope and evidence |
|---|---|---|
| ARCH | Architecture/source review | S2; dependency boundaries, actual toolchain inventory, official-contract citations, decisions and gaps. No runtime capability claim from source review alone. |
| CORE | Isolated core behavior checks | S3; independent temporary data directory; deterministic state transitions and request routing. Label any test doubles explicitly. |
| STORE | Persistence/schema tests | S3/S12; isolated SQLite/raw store, round-trip records, referential mapping, migrations, retention, redaction. Never open production agent stores. |
| CAP | Capability/contract tests | S2/S4–S6; compare UI controls against actual discovered backend options; contract tests may use labeled doubles but live handshake claims require real backend evidence. |
| CLAUDE-LIVE | Official Claude live acceptance | S4/S13; explicit non-sensitive prompts in an isolated test repo/session, streaming timestamps, actual session IDs, cwd, tool/cancel evidence, UI observations. |
| CODEX-LIVE | Official Codex live acceptance | S5/S13; explicit non-sensitive prompts in an isolated test repo/session; actual models/efforts, command stdout/stderr/exit, diff, test and cancel evidence. |
| DOT-LIVE | Authorized Dot live acceptance | S6/S13; official documented contract and authorized connection required. Missing contract/auth/private-API approval is BLOCKED; no fake backend substitution. |
| UI | Actual desktop interaction | S7/S13; launch and operate the real application, interact with controls, capture sanitized screenshots and action/result log. Source inspection or offscreen construction alone does not pass UI acceptance. |
| PROJECT | Project/session/task checks | S8; two isolated test projects and drafts; session mapping, metadata, safe switching, Git read-only state. Verify real backend cwd separately. |
| HANDOFF | Handoff integrity checks | S9; exact content and independent annotations, required metadata, preview/cancel/send paths. Actual cross-agent receipt requires LIVE-FINAL or relevant live suites. |
| HISTORY | Timeline/history/search checks | S10; isolated data with known messages/events; verify every filter and search domain and offline history. |
| VIEWS | Command/diff/test/artifact checks | S11; use isolated repo changes and real harmless command/test results; interact with unified and side-by-side viewer, copy/save/open boundaries. |
| RECOVERY | Crash/restart/disconnect checks | S12/S13; isolated application data; verify durable state after closure/crash, restoration, no auto-send, no silent new session. |
| SECURITY | Security boundary checks | S12/S13; synthetic secret sentinels only; inspect isolated DB/raw/export/diagnostics for leakage, confirm no hidden reasoning, auto-approval, bypass or production access. |
| LEGACY | Legacy preservation/integration checks | S1/S7; read only C:\Users\ryant\Desktop\AI-Collab-Monitor and evidence. Preserve files/readers/panes/session processes; use copied/synthetic logs for import mutation tests. |
| EXPORT | Export content checks | S10–S12; export isolated task to each required format, read back content/artifacts/metadata, scan synthetic-secret exclusions, verify archive paths. |
| DIAGNOSTICS | Diagnostics checks | S12; inspect UI diagnostic fields and exported ZIP using sanitized isolated records. |
| PERF | Performance/incremental-reader checks | S12/S13; record idle CPU measurement window and actual result; interact during concurrent streaming; stress only isolated generated JSONL, including partial lines and rotation. |
| PACKAGE | Desktop install/launch checks | S7/S13; approved per-user app data, launchable program, Desktop/Start menu shortcut, no system-level install or admin action without approval. |
| DOCS | Documentation/delivery review | S2–S13; inventory all required documents/source/tests/reports, check consistency with measured capabilities, preserve exact source PRD and explicit blockers. |
| LIVE-FINAL | Final combined acceptance | S13; all three real official/authorized backends operate simultaneously in isolated tasks via actual UI; all required interactions and integrity/recovery/security pass. Unit doubles never fulfill this suite. |

## Individual acceptance criteria

Each criterion below carries the reviewed frozen-source locations and its current evidence disposition. Fields listed separately are independently inspectable acceptance aspects. Lists or example values in the PRD do not authorize hardcoding backend choices. Features described as optional remain conditional; verify their boundaries if included. A missing mandatory local feature is FAIL after verification, not UNSUPPORTED.

## Architecture recommendations from requirements review

These recommendations are implementation guidance, not new product scope or evidence of completion.

1. Use `UI → Workspace Core → common adapter contract → official backend`. Keep adapter transport, persistent storage and view models independent. A PySide6 UI can use the same core contract if the existing toolchain cannot build Tauri without a system installation.
2. Let each adapter report capabilities and evidence provenance after a real handshake. Track transport availability separately from authenticated readiness. Never mark Connected from executable detection, and never populate selectable models or efforts from illustrative PRD examples.
3. Separate append-only, redacted raw event records from normalized display events. Assign stable event/session/task IDs. Apply one redaction and hidden-reasoning exclusion boundary before storage, diagnostic output, UI rendering, clipboard or export. Record redaction markers and reasons, never original secrets.
4. Persist projects, tasks, session mappings, messages, handoffs and settings transactionally in SQLite. Record a handoff's source, forwarded payload and annotation independently. A saved handoff does not prove the target received it; record preparation, confirmation, dispatch and receipt states distinctly.
5. Stream backend events on worker threads or asynchronous transports; marshal bounded incremental updates into the UI. Handle partial JSONL lines and rotation by offset/identity. Keep historical data readable when offline. Restore state after a crash without reconnecting, creating sessions or resending prompts automatically.
6. Implement Stop only where an official cancellation mechanism is verified. Surface Unsupported or Blocked with the exact reason if cancellation is absent. Do not use taskkill or process-tree termination as a substitute for an agent cancellation contract.
7. Treat a Dot adapter without a documented authorized transport as a visible blocked adapter, retaining task metadata/history capabilities only to their truthful extent. Complete independent core/UI/Claude/Codex work and report the real gap; do not declare the simultaneous three-agent Final gate complete.
8. Restrict file/artifact operations to selected project/test/data roots and explicit user selections. Use read-only Git commands. Stage shortcuts and installers in the workspace; perform per-user installation only within the existing authorization and platform permission boundary.
9. Keep legacy Monitor integration read-only and opt-in, recording source paths and incremental offsets. Empty legacy logs are a valid state, not synthetic evidence of historical activity.
10. Maintain two evidence layers: focused local tests for data/core/security, and real backend/UI acceptance in isolated test repositories. The Final Acceptance Report must reconcile every required gate with this tracker and clearly identify any untested, failed, unsupported or blocked condition.

## Coverage snapshot

- Numbered PRD sections: **100**.
- Individual PRD acceptance aspects: **653**.
- Additional execution constraints: **20**.
- PRD criteria: **396 PASS; 0 FAIL; 56 BLOCKED; 4 UNSUPPORTED; 197 PENDING**.
- Execution directives: **17 PASS; 1 BLOCKED; 2 PENDING**.
- Final gate: **BLOCKED / NOT ACCEPTED**.
- Machine-readable full mapping with exact check names, scopes and current/historical source hashes: [requirement-coverage.json](../evidence/requirement-coverage.json).
- Combined suite: **124 discovered, 122 PASS, 0 failures/errors, 2 opt-in Codex live skips**. Breakdown: Core 44, security 11, inspection 16, Claude 14, Codex 19 plus 16 schema/native-preview fixtures, recovery 2. The skipped opt-in suites passed separately against real Codex (16 checks).
- Claude: **13 official live checks PASS**, separate real text/PNG attachment PASS, and **7 official native SDK checks PASS**. The earlier OAuth failure is historical and superseded. Runs used isolated data, original permissions and no automatic approvals.
- UI: **22 authored, 13 actually executed, 12 PASS, 1 FAIL**, 0 errors/skips. The only visible run used the earlier 13-case source. R019.08 remains FAIL; subsequent fixes and added tests were not rerun.
- The visible run used Windows Qt and FakeCore, with no backend, production repo or network. Its UI assertions are not real-backend acceptance. Historical execution rejection evidence remains retained; it no longer means no UI was executed.
- A prior authorized `python run.py` startup and the visible test runner do not certify the compiled launcher, installation or shortcuts. The `.exe` and installer were not run.
- Local core tests include actual isolated subprocess crash recovery and synthetic load measurement; they do not certify GUI/three-backend performance.
- Native Claude discovery/history passed the scoped official SDK run; desktop native-session import/preview remains untested. Screenshot Core import/export and optional reviewed ZIP inclusion pass; default ZIP excludes pixels and automatic image-secret redaction is not claimed.
- Codex file-read/write/patch/diff protocol fixtures pass only their contract scope. R008.10–R008.13 and R073.11 remain PENDING for complete native-effect/desktop acceptance. Strict separate stdout/stderr remains UNSUPPORTED.
